**[00:11:47.950] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000065)  
Allow me to say my opening remarks.

**[00:11:52.750] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000066)  
To our distinguished chairperson, Kong Mika Swansing,

**[00:11:55.990] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000067)  
my fellow vice chairperson and colleagues in the House of Representatives,

**[00:12:00.970] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000068)  
to our tourism secretary, Dita Angara Mathay,

**[00:12:04.330] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000069)  
and the hardworking team of the Department of Tourism,

**[00:12:07.610] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000070)  
good evening and welcome to the Department's budget briefing.

**[00:12:15.310] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000071)  
It is a privilege to preside over our deliberations on a sector that continues to generate jobs,

**[00:12:21.890] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000072)  
sustain local enterprises, strengthen communities,

**[00:12:25.530] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000073)  
and showcase to the world why they should love the Philippines,

**[00:12:29.190] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000074)  
while encouraging Filipinos and visitors alike to discover more to love across our country.

**[00:12:35.590] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000075)  
This evening, we have the opportunity to hear from the Department of Tourism,

**[00:12:37.530] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000076)  
and to hear from our newly appointed Tourism Secretary, Sec. Dita Angara Mathay,

**[00:12:41.730] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000077)  
who has taken on the important responsibility of leading this vital sector.

**[00:12:46.650] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000078)  
At the same time, it is our duty in Congress to constructively examine

**[00:12:50.470] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000079)  
whether the Department's proposed budget responds to the needs of Philippine tourism,

**[00:12:55.810] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000080)  
and whether every peso we invest translates into more visitors,

**[00:13:00.290] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000081)  
better infrastructure, stronger tourism enterprises,

**[00:13:03.210] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000082)  
and more opportunities for our people.

**[00:13:07.510] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000084)  
and the importance of this sector is clear from the numbers.

**[00:13:10.250] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000085)  
In 2025, tourism generated 2.27 trillion in tourism direct gross value added,

**[00:13:16.750] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000086)  
equivalent to 8.1% of our GDP, while supporting millions of Filipino jobs.

**[00:13:24.430] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000087)  
Tourism, therefore, is not merely about destinations and visitor arrivals.

**[00:13:29.190] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000088)  
It is a major contributor to our economy, and more importantly, to the livelihood of our people.

**[00:13:34.950] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000089)  
These figures remind us why today's economy is so important,

**[00:13:37.510] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000090)  
and why this budget deliberation matters.

**[00:13:40.270] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000091)  
We must ensure that the resources we provide to the Department

**[00:13:43.850] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000092)  
are commensurate with both the opportunities and the challenges facing Philippine tourism.

**[00:13:50.310] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000093)  
As a tourism advocate, this is a sector I've always felt deeply connected.

**[00:13:55.010] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000094)  
Coming from Sorsogon, I have seen firsthand how tourism can transform communities

**[00:13:59.670] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000095)  
and create opportunities for our people.

**[00:14:02.750] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000096)  
Kasama po ako sa mga naniniwala that we should invest more in Philippine tourism.

**[00:14:06.390] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000097)  
And so I look forward to a productive and constructive deliberation,

**[00:14:11.490] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000098)  
one where we recognize our gains, identify where we can do better,

**[00:14:15.450] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000099)  
and determine how Congress and the DOT can work together to make tourism

**[00:14:19.610] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000100)  
an even stronger driver of inclusive and sustainable growth.

**[00:14:24.570] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000101)  
With that, may I now invite Sec. Dita Angara-Mathai

**[00:14:28.570] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000102)  
to present the Department's proposed budget and priorities.

**[00:14:32.170] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000103)  
Madam Secretary, you may now proceed.

**[00:14:35.010] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000104)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:14:36.390] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000105)  
Good evening, Madam Chairperson,

**[00:14:40.470] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000106)  
Honorable Members of the Committee,

**[00:14:42.390] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000107)  
and Distinguished Legislators.

**[00:14:45.070] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000108)  
Tourism has been creating value for our economy and our people.

**[00:14:50.550] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000109)  
For Fiscal Year 2027,

**[00:14:52.950] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000110)  
we intend to turn this momentum into greater economic value

**[00:14:57.430] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000111)  
and opportunities for more Filipinos.

**[00:15:00.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000112)  
May I now present the Department of Tourism's performance and direction for Fiscal Year

**[00:15:06.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000113)  
2027.

**[00:15:07.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000114)  
First, our performance and accomplishments for 2025, second, the DOT's proposed budget

**[00:15:24.430] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000115)  
under the Fiscal Year 2027 National Expenditure Program, and third, the additional funding

**[00:15:31.710] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000116)  
requirements needed to sustain our momentum and drive the next phase of tourism growth.

**[00:15:41.030] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000117)  
Our mandate is to lead the planning, development, promotion and coordination of Philippine tourism.

**[00:15:48.310] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000118)  
We deliver this as one department.

**[00:15:51.190] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000119)  
The Central Office sets policy and strategic direction.

**[00:15:55.610] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000120)  
Our regional offices develop destinations and tourism products, while our foreign offices

**[00:16:01.730] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000121)  
build markets and generate international demand.

**[00:16:04.030] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000122)  
Our agencies and corporations extend the Department's work across the tourism ecosystem, providing

**[00:16:15.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000123)  
specialized support in infrastructure, heritage and destination development, dive tourism,

**[00:16:24.160] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000124)  
retirement, visitor services and tourism promotion.

**[00:16:28.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000125)  
In terms of funding, our NGAs are directly funded through appropriations in the GAA,

**[00:16:36.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000126)  
while our GOCCs generally operate under their corporate operating budgets.

**[00:16:42.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000127)  
For Fiscal Year 2027, both Duty Free Philippines and the Tourism Promotion Board have proposed

**[00:16:50.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000128)  
budgetary support under the GAA.

**[00:16:57.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000129)  
Our work is delivered through four core programs, with branding as a dedicated sub-program under

**[00:17:04.340] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000130)  
market and product development.

**[00:17:07.960] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000131)  
Tourism Policy and Planning sets the direction for the development of tourism in Philippine

**[00:17:11.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000133)  
Tourism Industry Training develops our people.

**[00:17:15.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000134)  
Standards Development and Enforcement ensures quality.

**[00:17:19.560] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000135)  
Market and Product Development turns our tourism assets into experiences that people

**[00:17:24.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000136)  
can visit, enjoy and spend on.

**[00:17:28.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000137)  
Branding Campaign brings these experiences to the market, strengthening the Philippine

**[00:17:33.860] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000138)  
tourism brand and converting interest into actual travel.

**[00:17:39.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000140)  
These programs connect strategy, people, quality, products and markets into one tourism development

**[00:17:47.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000141)  
system.

**[00:17:53.010] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000142)  
The programs must translate into measurable outcomes.

**[00:17:55.770] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000143)  
Tourism Direct Gross Value Added measures tourism's contribution to the economy.

**[00:18:03.310] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000144)  
Tourism expenditures show how visitor activity translates into spending.

**[00:18:09.710] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000145)  
Tourism Employment tells us how that activity creates jobs.

**[00:18:13.370] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000148)  
jobs. Tourism arrivals measure our ability to attract visitors to the Philippines. Together,

**[00:18:20.910] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000149)  
these indicators tell us not only how tourism is performing, but how much value it has created

**[00:18:27.990] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000150)  
for the country and for Filipinos. And the numbers show the scale of tourism's contribution to the

**[00:18:36.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000151)  
economy. In 2025, tourism directly generated 2.27 trillion in economic value, equivalent to 8.1%

**[00:18:46.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000152)  
of our GDP. Tourism spending was also substantial, with 3.26 trillion pesos generated by domestic

**[00:18:55.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000153)  
tourism and 698 billion pesos from international visitors. This shows that tourism is a major

**[00:19:04.160] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000154)  
economic sector that drives tourism.

**[00:19:06.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000155)  
Tourism spending generates jobs and supports businesses and livelihoods across the country.

**[00:19:14.290] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000156)  
Let's look closely into the economic value of tourism, particularly to the creation of job

**[00:19:20.830] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000157)  
opportunities and livelihoods for millions of Filipinos. In 2025, tourism supported 7.7 million

**[00:19:29.530] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000158)  
jobs, representing 15.7% of total employment in the country. That is nearly

**[00:19:37.950] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000160)  
One in every six working Filipinos with tourism employment continuing to grow from 5.4 million in

**[00:19:44.930] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000161)  
2021. That economic activity is supported by continued tourism demand. From January to July

**[00:19:55.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000162)  
2026, international visitor arrivals reached 3.93 million, up by 1.5% from 3.87 million

**[00:20:06.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000163)  
during the same period last year. The United States,

**[00:20:10.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000164)  
of course, remains our largest source market, followed by South Korea and Japan, while China

**[00:20:18.080] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000165)  
has returned to make top-five markets. The challenge now is to build on these key markets

**[00:20:24.340] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000166)  
and accelerate international tourist arrivals through the rest of the year.

**[00:20:32.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000167)  
The current performance builds on the recovery achieved over the past three years. Visitor

**[00:20:39.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000168)  
arrivals increased from 5.48 million in 2023

**[00:20:45.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000169)  
to 6.48 million in 2025,

**[00:20:48.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000171)  
representing overall growth of 18.4 percent.

**[00:20:51.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000172)  
Sustaining that upward trajectory now requires us to compete more effectively for a larger

**[00:20:57.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000173)  
share of the regional tourism market.

**[00:21:00.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000174)  
But our performance must also be viewed with an increasingly competitive ASEAN tourism

**[00:21:07.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000175)  
market.

**[00:21:08.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000176)  
In 2025, the Philippines ranked 6th in ASEAN, improving from 7th place in 2024.

**[00:21:17.140] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000177)  
Yet, with 6.48 million arrivals, we captured only 5 percent of the regional market shown

**[00:21:24.800] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000178)  
here.

**[00:21:26.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000179)  
Our improved ranking is encouraging, but the opportunity is much larger.

**[00:21:32.020] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000180)  
Our task ahead is to compete for a greater share of ASEAN tourist arrivals.

**[00:21:37.080] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000181)  
Growing Tourism Market

**[00:21:39.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000182)  
Beyond tourist volume, our objective is to increase the economic value generated by every

**[00:21:48.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000183)  
visit.

**[00:21:49.800] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000184)  
The Philippines remained 5th in ASEAN in tourism receipts in 2025, generating about 12.15

**[00:21:57.480] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000185)  
billion U.S. dollars.

**[00:21:59.620] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000186)  
While receipts declined slightly from 2024, our regional position was maintained despite

**[00:22:06.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000187)  
higher travel costs.

**[00:22:09.040] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000188)  
and spending in selected categories.

**[00:22:12.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000189)  
The opportunity now is to attract more visitors and generate greater value from every visit.

**[00:22:19.100] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000190)  
Our regional position must also be viewed in the context of the resources behind tourism

**[00:22:27.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000191)  
development.

**[00:22:28.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000192)  
In 2025, the Philippines operated with about 49.7 million dollars, significantly below

**[00:22:36.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000193)  
the tourism budget of the major ASEAN destinations shown here.

**[00:22:40.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000194)  
Yet, tourism already contributes 2.27 trillion to our economy and supports 7.7 million Philippine

**[00:22:50.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000195)  
jobs.

**[00:22:51.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000196)  
We are emphasizing that greater strategic investment in tourism can unlock a much larger

**[00:22:57.960] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000197)  
opportunity as it can bring more tourists, generate more spending, create more jobs,

**[00:23:04.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000198)  
and spread socio-economic growth across our regions.

**[00:23:11.490] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000199)  
The investment opportunity becomes even more important.

**[00:23:12.490] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000200)  
The opportunity becomes even clearer when we look at tourism marketing budgets of ASEAN

**[00:23:17.210] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000201)  
member states.

**[00:23:19.070] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000202)  
In 2025, the Philippines invested 86.68 million dollars below several ASEAN competitors while

**[00:23:28.190] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000203)  
generating 74,758 tourist arrivals for every 1 million dollars in government marketing

**[00:23:37.090] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000204)  
investment.

**[00:23:38.090] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000205)  
One might say that Vietnam's smaller government branding budget is relatively lower than the Philippines,

**[00:23:44.090] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000206)  
but it may be well to note that the Vietnam's budget is largely complemented by strong private sector investment in tourism development, particularly the Sun Group.

**[00:23:58.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000207)  
The gains we have achieved so far provide a strong foundation for pursuing that opportunity.

**[00:24:04.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000208)  
Behind these numbers are concrete interventions that strengthen our people, destinations, tourism products, and access to markets.

**[00:24:15.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000209)  
Let me now highlight some of the department's key accomplishments for 2025 and 2026.

**[00:24:24.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000210)  
We begin with the people who deliver the tourism experience.

**[00:24:29.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000211)  
As of July 2026, the department has trained 52,960.

**[00:24:36.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000212)  
The department has trained 168 participants nationwide, with more than half coming directly from the tourism and hospitality industry.

**[00:24:44.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000213)  
We might have a slight reduction in number of trained participants this year due to recalibration and change in targeted participants,

**[00:24:52.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000214)  
but it is noteworthy that by strengthening the capabilities of our workforce, academe, and LGUs,

**[00:25:00.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000215)  
we build the service quality and local capacity needed for a more competitive tourism.

**[00:25:06.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000216)  
Apart from training, we also highlight the importance of accreditation of tourism enterprises and frontliners as it protects the quality of the tourism experience.

**[00:25:21.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000217)  
DOT-accredited tourism enterprises and frontliners increased by 35% from 11,545 in 2023 to 15,592 as of June 2026.

**[00:25:38.960] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000218)  
This year, may I note that we are going beyond accreditation numbers by strengthening compliance, monitoring, renewal, and service standards.

**[00:25:49.740] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000219)  
All these ensure that accreditation becomes a stronger mark of quality and confidence in Philippine tourism.

**[00:26:01.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000220)  
Quality tourism products must also be connected to the markets that sustain them.

**[00:26:06.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000221)  
As of September 2026, 20 new international routes have been created.

**[00:26:11.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000222)  
These routes have been launched, opening more gateways to destinations across the country.

**[00:26:17.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000223)  
Another 13 routes are set to launch, bringing projected additional capacity to more than 472,000 seats by year-end.

**[00:26:28.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000224)  
Beyond expanding access, we are positioning the Philippines in high-value tourism markets.

**[00:26:35.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000225)  
After the Philippines was recognized as the world's number one retirement destination,

**[00:26:41.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000226)  
the Philippine Retirement Authority,

**[00:26:43.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000227)  
saw average SRRV applications jump from 15 to 25 per day,

**[00:26:50.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000228)  
a 67% increase following the recognition.

**[00:26:55.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000229)  
The opportunity is significant.

**[00:26:58.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000230)  
In July 2026, Bank of Singapore reported that the global longevity economy,

**[00:27:05.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000231)  
driven by the growing spending power of older and retired consumers,

**[00:27:09.680] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000232)  
is valued at $3 trillion and projected to reach,

**[00:27:13.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000233)  
$5.4 trillion in 2034.

**[00:27:15.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000234)  
Travel and leisure are identified among the sectors positioned to benefit from this growth.

**[00:27:24.060] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000235)  
As the world's top-ranked retirement destination,

**[00:27:26.620] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000236)  
the Philippines is well-positioned to capture a greater share of this growing global market.

**[00:27:33.020] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000237)  
We are also building on our global leadership in dive tourism,

**[00:27:39.780] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000238)  
one of the Philippines' strongest high-value tourism products.

**[00:27:45.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000240)  
is estimated to generate $1.3 billion in tourism revenue, supported by our world-class marine

**[00:27:53.160] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000241)  
biodiversity, in seven consecutive years as the world's leading dive destination.

**[00:27:59.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000242)  
But the opportunity is larger. The Philippine Commission on Sports, School with Diving,

**[00:28:06.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000243)  
has identified 29 dive destination clusters and 956 dive sites nationwide.

**[00:28:15.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000244)  
Many of which are still emerging or undergoing development and validation.

**[00:28:21.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000245)  
Even under a conservative growth scenario, annual dive tourism revenue is projected to reach

**[00:28:28.020] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000246)  
$100.5 billion by 2030, from $73 billion in 2023.

**[00:28:35.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000247)  
BCSSD has certified 330 dive professionals and individuals as of June 2026.

**[00:28:45.260] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000249)  
This builds on the strong expansion in certifications since 2023 and reflects the continued development

**[00:28:52.100] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000250)  
of the skilled workforce needed to support a safe, professional, and competitive Philippine dive industry.

**[00:28:59.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000251)  
We are also expanding cruise tourism as a gateway to multiple Philippine destinations

**[00:29:05.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000252)  
within a single journey. Cruise calls recovered to 136 in 2025 and are projected to reach

**[00:29:14.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000253)  
155 calls across 38 destinations by 2027, with bookings already extending into 2028.

**[00:29:24.800] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000254)  
The opportunity is to convert each port call into greater local economic activity,

**[00:29:30.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000255)  
to shore excursions, longer stays, and onward travel.

**[00:29:36.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000256)  
Growth is already extending beyond traditional gateways, with cruise calls in the Visayas projected to double,

**[00:29:43.740] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000257)  
from 14 in 2026 to 28 in 2027, while new destinations are being assessed for future itineraries.

**[00:29:54.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000258)  
Supporting this growth is the Cruise Visa Waiver Program, which makes it easier

**[00:30:00.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000263)  
for cruise passengers to enter and experience the Philippines.

**[00:30:04.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000264)  
Since its launch in 2024, 16,563 waivers have been issued as of June 2026,

**[00:30:13.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000265)  
with 99.8% issued to Chinese nationals.

**[00:30:18.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000266)  
Notably, the 7,066 waivers issued in the first half of 2026

**[00:30:24.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000267)  
already exceeded the full year total for 2025.

**[00:30:30.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000268)  
The strong uptake demonstrates the importance of streamlined travel facilitation

**[00:30:35.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000269)  
in unlocking not just the Chinese cruise market,

**[00:30:39.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000270)  
but all our key and emerging markets by supporting further cruise tourism growth.

**[00:30:45.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000271)  
We are seeing the same conversion from global recognition

**[00:30:50.580] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000272)  
to economic activity in gastronomy tourism.

**[00:30:54.620] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000273)  
The inaugural Michelin Guide Philippines recognized 108 establishments,

**[00:31:00.120] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000274)  
placing our culinary seasonings,

**[00:31:01.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000275)  
following the Michelin Guide announcement,

**[00:31:06.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000276)  
DOT partner payment network data showed transactions increased by 33.6%,

**[00:31:14.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000277)  
while transaction value grew by 22.6% in the fourth quarter of 2025,

**[00:31:21.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000278)  
compared with the same period of 2024.

**[00:31:24.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000279)  
Filipino cuisine is becoming more than a tourism experience.

**[00:31:28.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000280)  
It is becoming a reason to travel to the Philippines.

**[00:31:31.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000281)  
The global recognition is helping us bring travelers beyond our already established destinations,

**[00:31:42.800] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000282)  
such as Boracay.

**[00:31:44.680] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000283)  
Emerging destinations are gaining international attention

**[00:31:48.140] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000284)  
and recording significant growth.

**[00:31:51.060] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000285)  
Tourist arrivals increased by 302% in Batanes and 110% in Camiguin,

**[00:31:57.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000286)  
while Cavite and El Nido also posted strong gains,

**[00:32:02.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000287)  
following greater,

**[00:32:03.260] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000288)  
international visibility. Each recognized destination becomes a gateway to nearby places,

**[00:32:10.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000289)  
inviting travelers to explore farther and discover a thousand more reasons to love

**[00:32:16.480] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000290)  
the Philippines. The Philippines' rise among non-OIC destinations in the 2026 Global Muslim

**[00:32:26.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000291)  
Travel Index demonstrates the results of our sustained investment in halal and Muslim-friendly

**[00:32:34.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000292)  
tourism. With 70 Muslim-friendly accommodation establishments nationwide, we are strengthening

**[00:32:41.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000293)  
destination readiness to diversify our source markets and capture a greater share of the

**[00:32:47.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000294)  
growing global Muslim travel market. We have strengthened our people, our standards, and

**[00:32:54.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000295)  
experiences and destinations that make the Philippines worth discovering. The next step

**[00:33:00.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000296)  
is to bring these stories to the market.

**[00:33:03.560] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000297)  
Through Discover,

**[00:33:04.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000298)  
More to Love, we bring together airlines, hotels, and tourism partners to encourage

**[00:33:10.340] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000299)  
Filipinos to explore more of the country. Here is a short video that provides a glimpse

**[00:33:16.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000300)  
of our Discover, More to Love, a refresh of Love, More, the Philippines.

**[00:33:22.860] Presentation video** (asr-000301)  
Have you ever received a gift thought was already amazing, only to discover there was even more

**[00:33:34.180] Presentation video** (asr-000302)  
waiting inside?

**[00:33:36.260] Presentation video** (asr-000303)  
That's the Philippines, familiar in name,

**[00:33:40.020] Presentation video** (asr-000304)  
but endless in discovery.

**[00:33:42.200] DOT Director Paulo Benito S. Tugbang** (asr-000304-split-2)  
Madam Chair, can we start the video again from the beginning so that we can hear the audio?

**[00:33:47.900] DOT Director Paulo Benito S. Tugbang** (asr-000304-split-3)  
The audio was in all of the first 10 seconds of the video. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[00:33:53.000] Presentation video** (asr-000305)  
Have you ever received a gift you thought was amazing, only to discover there was even

**[00:34:00.020] Presentation video** (asr-000306)  
more waiting inside?

**[00:34:01.740] Presentation video** (asr-000307)  
That's the Philippines.

**[00:34:04.420] Presentation video** (asr-000310)  
Ever found thanks and additional rewards?

**[00:34:05.780] Presentation video** (asr-000311)  
but endless and discovery beyond the familiar unfolding layer by layer are

**[00:34:14.480] Presentation video** (asr-000312)  
hidden codes unforgettable flavors and stories that reveal themselves one

**[00:34:20.900] Presentation video** (asr-000313)  
discovery at a time tourism partners across the country have come together to

**[00:34:27.260] Presentation video** (asr-000314)  
make every journey easier more affordable and more rewarding than ever

**[00:34:33.380] Presentation video** (asr-000315)  
so that every visit reveals something new and every new discovery feels a

**[00:34:39.680] Presentation video** (asr-000316)  
little like first love unexpected and impossible to forget introducing a new

**[00:34:49.850] Presentation video** (asr-000317)  
tourism platform your gateway to discover plan and book your journey and

**[00:34:57.790] Presentation video** (asr-000318)  
together who knows we all might just find the one discover more to love

**[00:35:08.340] Presentation video** (asr-000319)  
where every journey brings you closer to the one.

**[00:35:20.270] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000320)  
It's actually a play on the word love, Love the Philippines, like your first love and the one, so I think the

**[00:35:29.110] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000321)  
branding team did a good job on this video and we are already seeing the

**[00:35:34.990] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000322)  
conversion that conversion happened in the first 100 days discover more to love

**[00:35:41.290] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000323)  
generated more than two hundred three thousand

**[00:35:44.230] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000324)  
dates sold over 800 room nights and 1.79 million in documented hotel sales from

**[00:35:52.090] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000325)  
participating campaign partners more importantly these results show that

**[00:35:58.090] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000326)  
tourism promotion can move beyond visibility and translate directly into

**[00:36:02.650] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000327)  
travel bookings and local spending we are also investing in another powerful

**[00:36:09.190] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000328)  
way to connect the Philippines with travelers the Filipino talent

**[00:36:14.230] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000329)  
by engaging local artists and creative talents we strengthen both tourism

**[00:36:18.730] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000330)  
promotion and the country's creative economy in the first 100 days these

**[00:36:24.130] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000331)  
initiatives generated 51 000 active users 44 000 new users and 590 000 engagement across our tourism

**[00:36:35.590] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000332)  
platforms when Filipino talent carries the story of Philippine tourism we promote not only our

**[00:36:43.390] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000333)  
destinations

**[00:36:44.230] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000334)  
but the creativity and identity of the Filipino people in furtherance of RA number 11904 of the

**[00:36:52.630] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000335)  
Philippine creative Industries Development Act turning now to financial performance DOT close

**[00:37:02.410] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000336)  
fiscal year 2025 with an 86 percent utilization rate as of June 2026 utilization stood at 35

**[00:37:11.950] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000337)  
percent reflecting the strategic review of program priorities

**[00:37:17.050] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000338)  
and scheduling of major activities in the second semester with implementation now accelerating we

**[00:37:24.550] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000339)  
project utilization to reach 75 percent by September and full utilization by the end of the year

**[00:37:33.130] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000340)  
with that performance as backdrop allow me to present the Department of Tourism's fiscal year

**[00:37:39.130] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000341)  
2027 National expenditure program covering DOT OSEC and its attached agencies

**[00:37:47.050] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000342)  
under fiscal year 2027 NEP NEP the DOT has a total proposed budget of 5.47 billion pesos of this

**[00:38:00.240] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000343)  
4.03 billion covers DOT in its attached agencies while 1.45 billion provides budgetary support to

**[00:38:09.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000344)  
our government corporations these resources support the different but complementary rules of the DOT in

**[00:38:17.040] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000345)  
delivering our tourism agenda

**[00:38:18.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000346)  
on this note we humbly submit that our attached agencies and corporations deliver sufficient public

**[00:38:26.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000347)  
value relative to their fiscal costs based on their statutory tourism related mandates which

**[00:38:33.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000348)  
the heads of our attached agencies and corporations are ready to substantiate before the honorable

**[00:38:39.780] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000349)  
committee for fiscal year 2027 the DOT and its attached agencies have a proposed

**[00:38:48.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000350)  
budget of 4.03 billion under the NEP 4.64 percent lower than fiscal year 2026 JAA of 4.23 billion

**[00:38:58.800] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000351)  
pieces the overall reduction reflects lower allocations for the DOT office of the secretary

**[00:39:06.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000352)  
secretary in Tomura's administration and the national parks development committee

**[00:39:12.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000353)  
notably PCSSD is the only agency with an increase rece visible

**[00:39:18.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000354)  
49.95 million, up 11.23 percent, signaling continued support for the growth and development

**[00:39:27.020] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000355)  
of Philippine dive tourism. Despite the lower overall funding level, the Department is expected

**[00:39:34.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000356)  
to optimize available resources and prioritize investments that deliver the greatest impact

**[00:39:41.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000357)  
on tourism development and growth. In particular, of the 3.53 billion proposed for DOT, OSEC,

**[00:39:50.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000358)  
2.45 billion, or nearly 70 percent, is directed to program operations. Another 1.02 billion pesos

**[00:39:59.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000359)  
supports general administration, while 59 million is allocated for support to operations.

**[00:40:07.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000360)  
Overall, the fiscal year 2027, DOT,

**[00:40:11.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000361)  
OSEC budget is 4.64 percent lower than fiscal year 2026, GA, requiring greater focus and

**[00:40:20.780] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000362)  
efficiency in the use of our resources. For our core programs, of the 2.45 billion pesos

**[00:40:28.240] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000363)  
for operations, the largest allocation is for market and product development at 1.60

**[00:40:34.680] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000364)  
billion pesos, including 1 billion for the branding campaign. The remaining resources

**[00:40:40.680] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000365)  
support

**[00:40:41.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000366)  
Policy and planning, workforce development, and standards enforcement. Together,

**[00:40:47.260] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000367)  
these investments strengthen both tourism demand and industry readiness.

**[00:40:52.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000368)  
Let me now highlight our major investment priorities for fiscal year 2027, beginning

**[00:40:58.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000369)  
with the 1 billion branding campaign. To draw more tourists, compete effectively in the

**[00:41:04.740] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000370)  
international market, and capture a larger share of the ASEAN tourism market, sustained investments

**[00:41:10.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000371)  
investments in tourism marketing is

**[00:41:11.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000372)  
essential.

**[00:41:12.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000375)  
Thus, half of the indicative budget will strengthen and sustain the Philippine tourism brand through global media, partnerships and stronger performance measurement. The other half will focus on end-to-end conversion, moving travelers from awareness and interest to actual travel and spending.

**[00:41:36.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000376)  
Our next priority is investing in the

**[00:41:38.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000377)  
people who deliver our tourism experience.

**[00:41:40.240] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000378)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[00:41:40.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000379)  
year 2027, we will bring quality training closer to our regions, strengthen certification

**[00:42:31.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000387)

**[00:42:37.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000388)

**[00:42:38.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000389)

**[00:42:49.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000391)

**[00:42:57.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000392)  
Our next request is 22 million pesos for the development and review of three National Tourism

**[00:43:05.120] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000393)

**[00:43:08.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000394)

**[00:43:14.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000395)

**[00:43:21.120] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000396)

**[00:43:28.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000397)

**[00:43:35.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000398)  
as they provide the continuity and long-term direction needed to guide tourism policies,

**[00:43:41.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000399)

**[00:43:47.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000400)  
pesos to support the operations of our foreign offices. These offices are our frontline presence

**[00:43:54.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000401)

**[00:44:00.560] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000402)

**[00:44:04.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000403)  
and international partners.

**[00:44:14.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000405)

**[00:44:23.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000406)

**[00:44:30.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000407)

**[00:44:35.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000409)

**[00:44:44.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000410)

the next slide shows how the additional funding will be utilized for fiscal year 2027 the

and western visayas with 4.58 million paces available under the special provision we

these

investments require immediate attention as they can ensure better data stronger standards and

local capacity to showcase more competitive tourism destinations in summary we respectfully

that support tourism investment and planning international market presence continuity of

for filipinos for fiscal year 2027 the ddot osec has a proposed budget of 3.53 billion

the results represented today show what tourism can deliver when we invest strategically stronger

destinations and connectivity growing markets more tourism activities and greater economic

opportunities for filipinos as these initiatives gain traction we respectfully seek the committee's

**[00:48:55.710] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000446)

**[00:49:06.270] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000447)

**[00:49:14.880] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000448)

**[00:49:23.380] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000449)

**[00:49:29.940] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000450)

**[00:49:37.580] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000451)

**[00:49:41.060] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000452)

**[00:49:48.400] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000453)  
Projects. These investments will improve tourism facilities in Banaue, San Vicente, Coron,

**[00:49:56.680] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000454)

**[00:50:03.360] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000455)

For PCSSD, we are requesting a total amount of P82,490,000 to strengthen the country's dive tourism ecosystem.

**[00:50:49.470] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000459)  
The funding will support digitalization and industry data, dive safety and emergency response, regulatory compliance, institutional capacity, and sustainable destination development.

**[00:51:04.490] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000460)  
Reinforcing the Philippines' position as the world's leading dive destination.

**[00:51:11.730] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000461)  
In total, we are requesting P1.64 billion in additional funding for our five attached agencies.

**[00:51:26.370] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000462)  
The proposed funding will support priority tourism infrastructure, heritage, and parks development.

**[00:51:34.150] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000463)  
Dive tourism.

**[00:51:34.490] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000464)  
Tourism, culture development, and destination enhancement.

**[00:51:39.210] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000465)  
Strengthening the capacity of our attached agencies to deliver their mandates and advance national tourism priorities.

**[00:51:47.070] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000466)  
Overall, the department is seeking P1.732 billion in additional funding for fiscal year 2027, covering the P82.33 million for DOT OSEC and P1.65 billion

**[00:52:05.860] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000470)  
for the attached agencies.

**[00:52:07.620] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000471)  
These resources will address priority funding gaps and strategic requirements to sustain tourism programs, strengthen destinations, and support continued industry growth.

**[00:52:22.540] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000472)  
As we commemorate the World Tourism Day this September 27, we are reminded that tourism has always been a shared endeavor.

**[00:52:33.300] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000473)  
For 75 years, the call has remained.

**[00:52:36.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000475)  
remain the same. Government, private sector, and every Filipino have a role in advancing

**[00:52:44.220] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000476)  
Philippine tourism. Today, that shared responsibility is more important as we

**[00:52:49.700] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000477)  
harness technology, creativity, and innovation to make our destinations more competitive and

**[00:52:56.100] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000478)  
our growth more inclusive. With all of us working together, tourism can continue to be a powerful

**[00:53:02.880] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-000479)  
force for national development and a source of opportunity and pride for every Filipino.

**[00:53:09.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000480)  
Madam Chairperson, Honorable Members, and Distinguished Legislators, Philippine tourism

**[00:53:18.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000481)  
has demonstrated what it can deliver for our economy, our communities, and our people.

**[00:53:25.160] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000482)  
With your continued support, we can build on these gains, unlock the full potential of Philippine

**[00:53:31.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000483)  
tourism, and create a better Philippines.

Ban.

**[01:35:23.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001512)

**[01:35:24.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001513)

**[01:35:37.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001516)

So,

**[01:35:54.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001520)

**[01:36:00.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001521)

**[01:36:07.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001522)  
so, hindi masyadong mataas ang government investment ng Vietnam,

**[01:36:12.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001523)

**[01:36:16.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001524)

**[01:36:19.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001525)

**[01:36:24.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001526)  
Ah, dito po sa bensang ito.

**[01:36:26.460] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001527)  
Sek, dita, you are recognized?

**[01:36:28.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001528)  
Um, the Sun Group actually not only invests in marketing, they invest in the entire ecosystem.

**[01:36:36.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001529)  
So, yung tourist attractions, yung hotel, yung accommodations, yung buong village, yung buong barangay, nilalagyan nila ng pondo.

**[01:36:45.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001530)

**[01:36:54.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001531)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Madam Secretary.

**[01:36:57.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001532)  
Um, we support the Department of Tourism's call for augmentation sa kanilang budget,

**[01:37:02.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001533)  
talagang lalo na po yung panawagan doon sa, ano po ba yun, Tier 1?

**[01:37:05.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001534)  
Is that a Tier 1? Tier 2 request.

**[01:37:09.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001535)  
Um, kasi nga po, one out of six Filipinos are employed by the industry,

**[01:37:13.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001536)

**[01:41:44.710] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001605)  
for me because, um, maybe some of you are not, uh, familiar.

**[01:41:48.110] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001606)

**[01:41:49.730] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001607)  
Um, I wanted to bring it to slow food.

**[01:41:52.310] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001608)  
Um, and, uh, it's just a movement of farmers.

**[01:41:55.270] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001609)  
And it started in Italy in 1986.

**[01:41:57.290] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001610)  
And it's actually a protest, um, when McDonald's opened in Rome.

**[01:42:01.390] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001611)  
And it has grown to more than 160 countries.

**[01:42:04.210] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001612)  
Um, the idea is simple.

**[01:42:05.250] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001613)  
Food should be good.

**[01:42:06.270] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001614)  
It should be clean.

**[01:42:07.290] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001615)  
And the people who grow it should be paid fairly.

**[01:42:10.330] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001616)  
Uh, nakita ko po sa inyong presentation that, uh,

**[01:42:12.650] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001617)  
Chicken inasal is the number one chicken sa buong mundo.

**[01:42:15.650] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001618)  
Uh, marami pa pong pwedeng, uh, gastronomic, um, dishes, no, sa ating bansa.

**[01:42:21.330] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001619)  
And every so often, what they do is they gather all these farmers,

**[01:42:24.310] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001620)  
fish folks, and cooks in one place, and they call it Terramadre.

**[01:42:27.490] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001621)  
Uh, the big one is in Turin every two years.

**[01:42:29.610] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001622)  
In fact, it's coming up soon.

**[01:42:31.190] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001623)  
Uh, last November for the first time, um, they held it.

**[01:42:34.210] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001624)  
Uh, for Asia and Pacific, and they held it in Bacolod.

**[01:42:37.250] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001625)  
Uh, Madam Secretary and Madam Chair, there are 25 countries who came,

**[01:42:40.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001626)  
around 2,500 delegates, 75,000 visitors,

**[01:42:44.230] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001627)  
and after that, Slow Food International decided that Bacolod would be its hub for the region.

**[01:42:49.610] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001628)  
Now, I wanted to ask, um, because nung nakita ko po sa DUT volume,

**[01:42:53.350] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001629)

**[01:42:58.610] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001630)

**[01:43:04.210] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001631)  
uh, any line item, any program, any activity, uh, for Terramadre, Asia and Pacific,

**[01:43:09.970] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-001632)  
especially as, um, this scheduled for July next year po?

**[01:43:16.470] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001633)

**[01:43:42.590] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001638)

**[01:43:43.370] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001639)

**[01:43:51.550] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001640)

**[01:43:55.130] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001641)

**[01:44:00.610] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001642)

**[01:44:06.830] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001643)

First of all, Madam Chair, I would like to manifest my full support to the budget of the Department of Tourism.

**[01:48:52.590] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001703)  
Considering that I am from Dinagat and now living in Siargao, both tourism destinations,

**[01:49:02.770] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001704)  
nakikita-kita nga kami doon ni Mark sa Siargao.

**[01:49:08.530] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001705)  
Now, first of all, Madam Chair, I am alarmed with this issue paper of TIEZA.

**[01:49:19.590] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001706)  
Nandito pa po yung taga-DBM?

**[01:49:21.270] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001707)  
No.

**[01:49:22.590] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001708)  
Wala tayong from DBM?

**[01:49:34.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001709)  
May I ask, Madam Chair, DBM, bakit po outrightly dininayin ninyo yung request ng Tiesa for fund augmentation?

**[01:49:45.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001710)  
Alam naman po natin na hindi po maisusulong yung mga programa ng tourism kung wala tayong infrastructure.

**[01:49:58.800] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001711)  
Is there anyone from DBM?

**[01:50:04.930] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001712)  
Normally naman po, pagka-budget briefing, may taga-DBM.

**[01:50:08.250] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001713)  
Ma'am, you're acknowledged. Kindly state your name.

**[01:50:11.470] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001714)  
Good evening, Madam Chair. Your Honor, I'm Lulu Vispo, [unclear] of the Department of Budget and Management, handling TIEZA.

**[01:50:27.750] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001715)  
May we highlight, Your Honor, that the Tiesa's proposal for national government subsidy in FY2027

**[01:50:36.030] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001716)  
is in view of several legislative measures proposing the abolition of the travel tax.

**[01:50:41.330] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001717)  
However, as these bills remain under deliberation in the Senate,

**[01:50:46.490] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001718)  
yet to be enacted into law,

**[01:50:48.370] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001719)  
the Tiesa's requested subsidy was not considered in the FY2024 NEP.

**[01:50:54.290] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001720)  
And then, however, TIEZA may continue to utilize its travel tax collections to fund tourism-related infrastructure projects,

**[01:51:02.650] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001721)  
which is consistent with Sections 72 and 73 of RA No. 9593.

**[01:51:07.950] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001722)  
So, in short po, kasi wala pa pong approval na totally abolishing yung travel tax.

**[01:51:15.610] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001723)  
So...

**[01:51:16.490] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001724)  
Hindi po kami nag-recommend po.

**[01:51:20.330] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001725)  
Anyway, they still have their own or corporate operating budget po for 2027.

**[01:51:26.690] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001726)  
Madam Chair, may I ask the DBM, kapag ka hindi na, hindi na ipasa yung batas na yan at hindi na abolish yung tribal tax,

**[01:51:37.830] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001727)  
is there any legal impediment to augment the fund of Tiesa?

**[01:51:46.320] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001728)  
Madam Chair, Your Honor,

**[01:51:48.480] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001729)  
Tiesa may prioritize the utilization of its available fund,

**[01:51:53.360] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001730)  
totaling 2.355 billion, which means unobligated allotment of 810.250 as of March 31, 2026,

**[01:52:03.160] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001731)  
and 1.5 billion unreleased appropriations for FY2025 to finance the priority projects of Tiesa po.

**[01:52:14.560] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001732)  
So, meron po rin silang mga unobligated obligation.

**[01:52:18.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001733)  
Iyon na nga po, Madam Chair.

**[01:52:23.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001734)  
Nakikita po ba ng DBM yung pangangailangan ng infrastructure sa mga different tourist destination natin,

**[01:52:30.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001735)  
kagaya nung halimbawa nung mga airports, although saka po yun,

**[01:52:36.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001736)  
pero may mga maliliit po na bagay na kinakailangan doon mga airports, mga seaports natin na pinupundungan po ng Tiesa.

**[01:52:46.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001737)  
Hindi ko po maintindihan.

**[01:52:47.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001738)  
Bakit napakabilis po ninyong mag-release ng tira doon sa mga, for lack of a better term,

**[01:52:55.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001739)  
walang mga kabuluhang project na hindi nga directly nakakatulong sa employment?

**[01:53:03.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001740)  
Pasinsya na po kasi nakikita ko po yung malaking tulong ng tourism sa mga area na nangangailangan ng employment,

**[01:53:12.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001741)  
pero hindi ko maintindihan.

**[01:53:13.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001742)  
So, anyway, Madam Chair, maraming salamat po.

**[01:53:17.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001743)  
DBM doon sa paliwanag. Sana may parating mo kay Secretary Kim na wala naman pong legal impediment kung mag-augment tayo ng pundo.

**[01:53:26.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001744)  
Kahit kakapiranggot, may pakita lang natin na DBM is supporting our tourism industry.

**[01:53:35.300] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001745)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[01:53:36.220] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001746)  
But may we add also that TIEZA may also tap the SAGF of the TPB to support the development of tourism.

**[01:53:47.640] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001747)  
For enterprise zones po.

**[01:53:49.940] DBM Assistant Director Lulu P. Vispo** (asr-001748)  
This is consistent with Section 55 of RA No. 9593.

**[01:53:56.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001749)  
Sige po. Talagang binabarat nyo yung tourism.

**[01:54:03.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001750)  
Pagka pupunta po kayo ng Siargao, hindi ko kayo pasakayin ng fast craft.

**[01:54:10.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001751)  
Yung pinakamahina na sasakyan ng taga-DBM.

**[01:54:15.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001752)  
By the way,

**[01:54:18.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001753)  
Madam Chair, may I ask D.O.T., mayroon po ba kayong malalim na ugnayan sa D.N.R.?

**[01:54:25.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001754)  
Yung regular na ugnayan sa D.N.R.?

**[01:54:35.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001755)  
I'm asking that, Madam Chair, kasi I think the good Secretary will agree with me na hindi po pwedeng magkatabi yung mining at saka tourism.

**[01:54:49.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001756)  
That's the point I am raising.

**[01:54:53.680] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001757)  
Usec Majo, you are recognized.

**[01:54:56.400] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001758)  
Madam Chair, Your Honor, we have convergence program with them, with the D.N.R.

**[01:55:09.810] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001759)  
So we have what we call the Sustainable Tourism Program, which aim to protect and ensure the sustainability of Philippine tourism resources and attractions.

**[01:55:19.930] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001760)  
So we have partners under this program, D.N.R., the WWF Philippines and the NCCA.

**[01:55:27.270] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001761)  
And we have training areas.

**[01:55:29.990] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001762)  
Regularly, sustainable tourism, sustainable dining, and community eco-guiding.

**[01:55:36.750] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001763)  
So meron po kaming tulungan doon.

**[01:55:39.630] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001764)  
And we have conducted 13 trainings and 417 participants trained na po.

**[01:55:47.210] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001765)  
That's the collaboration we have under the convergence program, Your Honor, Madam Chair.

**[01:55:52.610] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001766)  
Madam Chair, maganda pong pakinggan yung programa na yon.

**[01:55:56.770] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001767)  
Pero ang tinutukoy ko po dito, paano nyo in-implement yan?

**[01:55:59.990] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001768)  
Alam nyo ba yung dinagat? Nakikita ba ng taga-regional office yung mukha ng mga kabundukan ng dinagat?

**[01:56:06.990] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001769)  
But we are promoting tourism there.

**[01:56:12.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001770)  
Alam nyo yung bonsai forest sa Loreto? That's a tourism destination?

**[01:56:17.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001771)  
Tapos bakit kung mayroon kayong malalim na ugnayan ng DENR, bakit hindi nyo nakita na naisyuhan ng mga MPSA yan?

**[01:56:26.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001772)  
Why did the Department of Tourism allow that?

**[01:56:36.570] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001773)  
Sec. Dita, you're recognized

**[01:56:38.870] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001774)  
Bukas po.

**[01:56:39.810] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001775)  
I'm going to speak in a heritage event. Ang kasama ko po si DENR [unclear]. Kakausapin ko po siya nang masinsinan po.

**[01:56:53.410] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001776)  
At sasabihin ko, kung hindi siya, hindi niya ako papansin, susumuko siya sa inyo.

**[01:57:00.550] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001777)  
Madam Chair, ako, I am a living witness, how tourism, no?

**[01:57:06.910] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001778)  
Help, no? Our employment.

**[01:57:09.810] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001779)  
Unemployment problem. Unemployment problem.

**[01:57:13.270] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001780)  
What I am recommending, Madam Chair, is that sana, you could have an inventory sa mga tourism destinations natin.

**[01:57:22.550] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001781)  
Talk with D.N.R., coordinate with D.N.R.

**[01:57:24.790] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001782)  
Dapat itong mga area na to, wag kayong isyo ng isyo ng...

**[01:57:28.730] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001783)  
Lahat, pagka sumakay ka ng aeroplano papuntang Siargao, nakikita mo pagdaan mo sa dinagat.

**[01:57:36.090] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001784)  
Wala na po yung baybayin ng dinagat.

**[01:57:38.570] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001785)  
Parehas lang po ng Siargao.

**[01:57:39.810] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001786)  
Siargao ang klase, pero ano na po siya.

**[01:57:45.190] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001787)  
Tingnan nyo na lang, yung sa regional office, tingnan nyo doon.

**[01:57:49.390] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001788)  
Opo.

**[01:57:51.070] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001789)  
Can I get a commitment from the good secretary na laliman nyo yung ugnayan nyo with D.N.R.

**[01:58:00.930] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001790)  
In particular, yung dinagat, which is, has a big potential, no?

**[01:58:07.670] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001791)  
As a next...

**[01:58:09.810] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001792)  
Tourism destination doon sa Caraga.

**[01:58:14.450] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001793)  
Yes, sir. You have our commitment. Thank you very much, po.

**[01:58:18.350] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001794)  
Wala na kong time, Madam Chair, sa plenary na lang.

**[01:58:23.590] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001795)  
Pero, in the proper time, I will support, no, Madam Chair, in the proper time, the augmentation of the budget of the DOT.

**[01:58:34.810] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001796)  
Most particularly, the TIEZA.

**[01:58:38.350] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001797)  
Thank you.

**[01:58:39.290] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001798)  
Thank you.

**[01:58:39.350] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001799)  
Thank you.

**[01:58:39.650] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001800)  
Thank you.

**[01:58:39.790] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001801)  
Thank you, Representative Dagooc.

**[01:58:41.170] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001802)  
Before we proceed, I would like to acknowledge the presence of Deputy Majority Leader Howard Guintu.

**[01:58:48.230] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001803)  
Next, to interpellate, from the majority, Representative Salvador Pleyto.

**[01:58:53.450] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001804)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:58:55.710] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001805)  
Magandang gabi po sa ating lahat.

**[01:58:57.930] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001806)  
Kay Secretary Matay, saka DOT family.

**[01:59:03.290] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001807)  
Madam Chair, it is being said that tourism cannot thrive.

**[01:59:13.690] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001808)  
thrive on beautiful destinations alone.

**[01:59:17.470] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001809)  
Natural appeal and rich culture are only half the equation.

**[01:59:22.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001810)  
Sustainable growth requires supporting physical infrastructure.

**[01:59:27.010] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001811)  
Kailangan po ng dependable roads, airports, ports, reliable water supply, proper sanitation, telecommunications, and stable electricity.

**[01:59:37.150] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001812)  
While the Department of Tourism leads marketing and destination development,

**[01:59:42.190] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001813)  
its success hinges on interagency alignment.

**[01:59:47.150] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001814)  
Without synchronized investment across basic services,

**[01:59:52.930] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001815)  
high potential sites across our regions will remain underutilized.

**[01:59:58.130] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001816)  
underperforming,

**[02:00:00.000] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001831)  
or inaccessible to both local and international tourists.

**[02:00:05.560] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001832)  
In view of that, I would like to seek clarification, Secretary,

**[02:00:08.900] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001833)  
from the Department on the following operational points.

**[02:00:13.060] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001834)  
May I ask Undersecretary Guillermo,

**[02:00:17.020] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001835)  
kasi narinig ko kanina na sumasagot tungkol dito sa aking tanong,

**[02:00:22.570] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001836)  
what specific tourism sites have been identified by the Department of Tourism

**[02:00:28.230] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001837)  
as high potential but whose visitor growth remains affected by inadequate infrastructure?

**[02:00:36.290] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001838)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:00:37.990] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001839)  
Usec Majo, you're recognized.

**[02:00:45.880] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001840)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[02:00:47.680] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001841)  
So, we have, as we have mentioned before,

**[02:00:51.360] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001842)  
we were looking into focusing,

**[02:00:55.280] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001843)  
well, the OT strategic direction is focused really on connectivity,

**[02:00:59.100] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001844)  
domestic tourism, destination readiness, investment, and ease of entry.

**[02:01:03.740] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001845)  
Our key transport projects include the new Manila International Airport in Bulacan

**[02:01:09.840] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001846)  
and the proposed railway connectivity between Bulacan and Clark.

**[02:01:14.040] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001847)  
So, we do acknowledge that it cannot solve infrastructure gaps alone.

**[02:01:18.380] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001848)  
So, different agencies have also distinct roles to address the gaps.

**[02:01:22.220] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001849)  
So, we have DPWH, we have convergence with them for the implementation of road construction

**[02:01:27.860] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001850)  
and then we also have the DOTr that implements airports,

**[02:01:33.380] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001851)  
ports and transport infrastructure development.

**[02:01:36.560] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001852)  
We also have the PIDS, the Philippine Institute for Development Studies,

**[02:01:44.040] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001853)  
which published a study citing how structural gaps,

**[02:01:48.120] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001854)  
particularly connectivity and infrastructure gaps,

**[02:01:50.940] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001855)  
continue to limit the growth of the Philippine sector.

**[02:01:54.580] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001856)  
Now, with this, Madam Chair, Your Honor,

**[02:01:58.800] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001857)  
we are looking at, of course, the Secretary,

**[02:02:03.380] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001858)  
in March 2026, approved the MOA,

**[02:02:07.380] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001859)  
designating Siquijor and Siargao as pilot sites for these infrastructure projects.

**[02:02:14.280] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001860)  
And also, in May 2025 to January 2026,

**[02:02:18.200] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001861)  
there was actually the drafting of that MOA

**[02:02:20.940] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001862)  
and the finalization of that MOA, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[02:02:27.460] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001863)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:02:29.340] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001864)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:02:30.040] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001865)  
Thank you, Secretary Yermo.

**[02:02:32.660] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001866)  
Madam Chair,

**[02:02:33.460] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001867)  
how does the DOT ensure that its priority destinations

**[02:02:38.260] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001868)  
are reflected in the National Expenditure Program allocations

**[02:02:43.040] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001869)  
of implementing agencies like DPWH and DOTR,

**[02:02:48.280] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001870)  
ensuring project execution keeps pace with promotional campaigns?

**[02:02:53.020] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001871)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:02:57.660] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001872)  
Anybody from the Undersecretary?

**[02:02:59.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-001873)  
Usec Jasmin, you're recognized.

**[02:03:01.100] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001874)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:03:02.480] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001875)  
In DOT proposed 28 projects,

**[02:03:06.400] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001876)  
amounting to approximately 6.02 billion

**[02:03:09.760] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001877)  
under its fiscal year 2027 Tier 2 proposal

**[02:03:14.500] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001878)  
after DPWH proposed that funding for continuing and new trip projects

**[02:03:19.760] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001879)  
be charged against DOT budget ceiling.

**[02:03:23.260] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001880)  
However, we all know that this was not approved for inclusion in the 2027 NEP.

**[02:03:30.800] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001881)  
So, this poses a very big problem for DOT

**[02:03:33.600] DOT Undersecretary Maria Victoria V. Jasmin** (asr-001882)  
because, you know, we're really looking at infrastructure,
