for interventions such as yung family food packs and emergency cash transfers.

**[02:13:40.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002072)  
Now, regarding naman po sa mga tinamaan po,

**[02:13:43.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002073)  
halimbawa nasira po yung tourism area,

**[02:13:46.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002074)  
sa kasawiang-palad po, wala pong pondo po kasi na inilalaan po ng NDRRMC para sa DOT

**[02:13:53.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002075)  
tulad ng ibang mga ahensya ngayong darating na fiscal year 2027.

**[02:13:58.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002076)  
Hinihiling po namin dati sa loob po ng, ah,

**[02:14:01.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002077)  
tier 2 po namin yung 5 million pesos to be incorporated po or to be carried po under the NDRRMC.

**[02:14:08.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002078)  
Pero hindi po ito, ah,

**[02:14:09.820] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-002079)  
pero hindi po ito naisama po dito nung lumabas na po yung NEP fiscal year 2027.

**[02:14:17.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002080)  
Ah, thank you Madam Chair.

**[02:14:19.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002081)  
Ah, kasi ang usaping ito ay hindi lamang po para sa ating mga,

**[02:14:22.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002082)  
sa amin sa Catanduanes, maraming tourism communities nationwide ang exposed din po sa disaster.

**[02:14:29.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002083)  
So, Madam Chair, sa tourism recovery hindi lang po destination ang kailangan ibangon,

**[02:14:35.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002084)  
kailangan din masuporta ng malilit na negosyo,

**[02:14:38.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002085)  
at tourism workers hanggang makabalik ang operasyon,

**[02:14:41.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002086)  
bumalik ang mga, bumalik ang mga turista at maging stable muli ang kanilang kabuhayan

**[02:14:47.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002087)  
dahil isa rin po sila sa mga tumutulong upang palaguin ang ating ekonomiya,

**[02:14:52.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002088)  
kung kaya narapat lang din po na tulungan natin sila.

**[02:14:56.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002089)  
Thank you po Madam Chair, thank you po Madam Secretary.

**[02:15:01.460] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-002090)  
Thank you, Rep. Rodriguez. Next, to interpellate from the minority, Representative Renee Co.

**[02:15:10.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002091)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, and good evening to the Department of Tourism.

**[02:15:17.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002092)  
Siguro, isa lang naman po yung tanong ko, and it relates to the duty-free shops of, well, the Philippines.

**[02:15:26.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002093)  
Ito, I was able to talk to some of my parliamentarian friends from other countries, and visit some duty-free shops din sa kanilang mga bansa.

**[02:15:41.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002094)  
And yun ay talagang opportunity to showcase yung local businesses, yung kanilang mga innovations, and culture.

**[02:15:52.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002095)  
And nakikita ko naman na ine-endeavor ng duty-free shops natin.

**[02:15:56.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002096)  
To do the same, lalo na at may historical importance at pagtangi din sa kanila.

**[02:16:04.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002097)  
Though, nabalitaan natin na yung essentially main mall ng ating duty-free shops, yung Fiesta Mall, ay magsasara na.

**[02:16:15.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002098)  
At magda-downsize sila sa isang office sa Paranaque.

**[02:16:19.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002099)  
May I ask po, ano po yung update doon? Bakit po nag-decide na i-close yung Fiesta Mall?

**[02:16:26.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002100)  
And bakit po lilipat yung office, yung duty-free shop natin?

**[02:16:33.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002101)  
May we refer this to COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco, Chief Operating Officer.

**[02:16:40.440] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-002102)  
COO Joel Cinco, you're recognized.

**[02:16:46.160] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002103)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Thank you, Madam Secretary. Good evening, Your Honor.

**[02:16:50.600] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002104)  
As to the concern po sa Fiesta Mall, just to clarify,

**[02:16:57.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002105)  
hindi po siya magsasara.

**[02:17:01.220] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002106)  
So, what happened po, just to give you a background,

**[02:17:06.460] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002107)  
kasi nag-expire yung lease agreement po noong last June.

**[02:17:14.440] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002108)  
So, we had an option to right-size, mainly because as to ano po, we're incurring—mataas ho kasi yung renta.

**[02:17:25.460] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002109)  
And for us to be able...

**[02:17:27.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002110)  
So, to cope up with the operating expenses,

**[02:17:33.720] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002111)  
in-identify po namin yung mga areas na pwede namin i-right size.

**[02:17:40.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002112)  
Since we are a self-sustaining agency.

**[02:17:43.660] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002113)  
So, ganun po yung ginawa.

**[02:17:44.640] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002114)  
So, and yung lessor po namin,

**[02:17:51.640] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002115)  
so, nung nag-expire po yung kontrata, yung lessor po namin,

**[02:17:55.280] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002116)  
there was...

**[02:17:57.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002117)  
there was a new upcoming lessor.

**[02:18:01.660] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002118)  
Ang plano po nila is to revive.

**[02:18:05.440] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002119)  
So, i-redevelop po ang Fiesta Mall.

**[02:18:09.400] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002120)  
So, magkakaroon po, kasi right now, kung mapapansin nyo,

**[02:18:13.800] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002121)  
nawala po yung mga kainan.

**[02:18:16.340] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002122)  
So, nawala po talaga ng activity yung area po.

**[02:18:20.640] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002123)  
So, with the new lessor po, ang plano po nila is really to redevelop.

**[02:18:27.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002124)  
I-revive yung complex.

**[02:18:30.060] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002125)  
And si Duty Free Philippines po ay magiging anchor tenant.

**[02:18:33.800] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002126)  
Which, yun po yung tinitingnan namin na anggulo whether to pursue.

**[02:18:40.780] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002127)  
But at this point po, tuloy-tuloy lang po yung operations ni Duty Free Philippines po sa Fiesta Mall.

**[02:18:47.760] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002128)  
At hindi po magsasara.

**[02:18:49.740] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002129)  
As to yung office naman po,

**[02:18:52.760] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002130)  
as part of our efforts to...

**[02:18:57.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002131)  
address the high-rising cost mo.

**[02:18:59.960] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002132)  
Kasi po before, yung complex composed of the...

**[02:19:04.400] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002133)  
may nandyan ho yung custom-bounded warehouse,

**[02:19:06.520] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002134)  
nandyan ho yung mall,

**[02:19:08.160] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002135)  
nandyan ho yung office.

**[02:19:09.900] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002136)  
So, ngayon po, ang ginawa ho namin is to decentralize.

**[02:19:14.060] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002137)  
So, yung office po na binanggit nyo na lilipat ho sa may Pasay.

**[02:19:21.380] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002138)  
So, those are the cost-effective measures na in-employed,

**[02:19:27.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002139)  
implement ho namin para ho maka-cope up si Duty Free Philippines po.

**[02:19:33.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002140)  
So, to clarify, COO and Madam Chair,

**[02:19:40.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002141)  
yung basically magkakaroon po ng ibang lessor.

**[02:19:47.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002142)  
And then, Duty Free will be instead paying rather than to kung sino man may-ari ng Fiesta Mall,

**[02:19:53.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002143)  
to this lessor.

**[02:19:54.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002144)  
Sino pong lessor ito?

**[02:19:56.000] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002145)  
It's a private.

**[02:19:57.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002146)  
It's a private entity who's willing to invest to redevelop the area.

**[02:20:05.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002147)  
And when you mean anchor-tenant,

**[02:20:08.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002148)  
yung character ng pagiging duty-free ng Fiesta Mall will maintain?

**[02:20:13.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002149)  
Or mag-iiba po ba yung essentially characteristics neto?

**[02:20:17.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002150)  
And isang bahagi na lang, kumbaga, yung duty-free ng i-redevelop na Fiesta Mall?

**[02:20:24.680] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002151)  
Essentially po, we will maintain.

**[02:20:27.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002152)  
Our, as an anchor-tenant.

**[02:20:30.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002153)  
But ang plano ho gawin, yung bagong lessor is to redevelop and complement

**[02:20:37.520] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002154)  
kung ano ho yung meron tayo right now sa duty-free.

**[02:20:40.800] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002155)  
So, ang na-relay ho sa amin na plano is magkakaroon ho ng mga F&Bs,

**[02:20:50.080] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002156)  
mga restaurant po, to augment the experience po nung complex.

**[02:20:56.000] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002157)  
At the same way, magkakaroon ho ng mga ibang tenants that would complement with us.

**[02:21:04.140] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002158)  
Hindi ho siya magiging competing para ho to lift up yung presence ng Fiesta Mall

**[02:21:14.740] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002159)  
at yung mga bago hong tenants na papasok.

**[02:21:18.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002160)  
So, to clarify, Madam Chair, kaya po ba may right-sizing dahil po is duty-free?

**[02:21:26.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002161)  
Is it too expensive now to maintain compared to the income that it can generate for the Philippines?

**[02:21:34.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002162)  
Ano pong considerations bakit po nag-right-size?

**[02:21:36.820] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002163)  
Nag-right-size po mainly because, kasi po before, we used to occupy the entire complex.

**[02:21:42.940] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002164)  
And we're not fully utilizing it.

**[02:21:45.740] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002165)  
So, with right-sizing, basically po, we're utilizing the right space.

**[02:21:56.000] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002166)  
Kasi ngayon, kung titignan natin, mas maganda ho kung namamaximize yung income, yung sales,

**[02:22:04.500] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002167)  
on the size, talaga hong requirement ni duty-free.

**[02:22:10.620] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002168)  
Kasi po dati, medyo malaki ho masyado.

**[02:22:14.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002169)  
Does duty-free have a right-sizing and asset disposition plan po ba, Madam Chair?

**[02:22:21.060] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002170)  
Regarding po sa, you mean sa space?

**[02:22:24.000] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002171)  
Sa buong ano po?

**[02:22:26.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002172)  
Yes, and even kahit sa iba pong mga duty-free shops, if applicable,

**[02:22:31.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002173)  
meron po bang ibang right-sizing efforts ngayon yung duty-free beyond yung sa Fiesta Mall?

**[02:22:36.960] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002174)  
At this point po, yung Fiesta Mall lang po yung nag-implement po kami ng right-sizing.

**[02:22:45.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002175)  
Okay. Kung meron pong plan, can this representation receive a copy through the committee?

**[02:22:51.460] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002176)  
Will do, Madam Chair.

**[02:22:53.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002177)  
Okay.

**[02:22:54.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002178)  
I bring this up also, Madam Chair, because we are aware that merong mga retrenched employees before and up until now may outstanding claims.

**[02:23:06.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002179)  
So, na-consider po ba kung paano babayaran yung claims na yun doon sa right-sizing na ginagawa?

**[02:23:12.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002180)  
Kaya po bang punan yung claim nila in the midst of not only right-sizing,

**[02:23:19.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002181)  
pero yung perception na nagbabayad ng masyadong malaki?

**[02:23:24.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002182)  
Okay. At hindi po na-utilize completely at therefore parang masayang kung tuloy-tuloy naman pong binabayaran yung Fiesta Mall?

**[02:23:33.740] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002183)  
Madam Chair, just to clarify po, the right-sizing pertains only to the space ng Fiesta Mall.

**[02:23:41.560] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002184)  
Though with the right-sizing po ng Fiesta Mall, wala hong affected na empleyado.

**[02:23:48.080] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002185)  
I think you're pertaining po yung previous po na mga na...

**[02:23:54.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002186)  
previously po, kasi yung nag-restructure noong 2023.

**[02:23:59.600] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002187)  
So, kami po ay proactively nag-re-reach out po doon sa remaining employees na na-separate

**[02:24:08.380] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002188)  
through issuing formal notices and extending HR assistance.

**[02:24:17.340] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002189)  
Primarily para ho mabayaran and ma-facilitate po yung mga naiwan ho ng mga...

**[02:24:24.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002190)  
compensation na hindi pa ho natatanggap.

**[02:24:28.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002191)  
Kung maaari na lang po, Madam Chair, makapagsubmit din ng report tungkol sa ano pong progress noon.

**[02:24:35.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002192)  
Ayaw naman po natin na dahil meron pa nga may outstanding claims na hindi mabayaran

**[02:24:41.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002193)  
if in case may financial difficulties yung duty fee.

**[02:24:45.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002194)  
But kung ang nire-report naman ay may right-sizing na nangyayari,

**[02:24:51.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002195)  
and sa dulo po nito,

**[02:24:54.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002196)  
kaya pa rin mabayaran yung outstanding claims,

**[02:24:56.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002197)  
we'd like to receive the following updates through our report.

**[02:24:59.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002198)  
Would that be possible po, Madam Chair?

**[02:25:02.980] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002199)  
Opo, Madam Chair, makakaasa ho kayo.

**[02:25:05.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002200)  
Magpibigay ho kami ng updates as to the settlement ng mga remaining po.

**[02:25:14.520] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002201)  
But definitely po, as to ano po, yung sa remaining ho na hindi pa ho nababayaran,

**[02:25:20.980] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002202)  
naka-earmark na ho yun.

**[02:25:22.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002203)  
So, may mga pending...

**[02:25:24.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002204)  
Resolutions na lang ho, and mga GSIS clearances, yun na lang naman po yung mga kulang.

**[02:25:30.580] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002205)  
But, naka-earmark na ho yan, kung ano ho yung mga yung resulta,

**[02:25:35.800] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002206)  
makakaasa ho kayo na magkocomply ho kami.

**[02:25:41.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002207)  
As a final question, Madam Chair, may partnerships po ba ang duty-free with local businesses,

**[02:25:51.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002208)  
with homegrown businesses of different regions,

**[02:25:54.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002209)  
na maaaring mas feature pa sa ating mga duty-free shops?

**[02:25:58.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002210)  
Ano po bang, essentially, business model na inooperating ngayon ng duty-free?

**[02:26:02.200] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002211)  
Opo, Madam Chair, ngayon po, tuloy-tuloy po ang pagtasok namin sa mga partnerships.

**[02:26:10.540] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002212)  
Mayroon ho kami mga B2B partnerships.

**[02:26:25.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002213)  
In fact, ano po, pumapartner ho kami sa mga hospitality...

**[02:26:30.900] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002214)  
Businesses, sa mga hotels, para ho, to help us promote yung duty-free.

**[02:26:39.340] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002215)  
As a, ano po, similarly, we've also partnered with mga...

**[02:26:47.100] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002216)  
Banking system, e-wallet companies, para ho to mapabilis yung mga transactions namin.

**[02:26:56.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002217)  
Also, we've also extended yung mga partnerships namin to...

**[02:27:00.900] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002218)  
Tourism organizations and government, other government agencies.

**[02:27:06.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002219)  
Primarily ho, to really help us promote and extend our reach throughout the Philippines.

**[02:27:13.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002220)  
Yun na lang din po yung final comment ko po siguro na marami pong mga,

**[02:27:18.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002221)  
at least for now, efforts I see sa DTI na mas ma-extend yung reach ng mga local businesses to national stages.

**[02:27:30.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002222)  
Kung maaari din ay maakit to sa international stage duty-free shops,

**[02:27:36.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002223)  
and kung paano pa mas mapatampok yung mga kakayahan ng mga Filipino brands and businesses,

**[02:27:44.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002224)  
maaari kong ma-explore yun, it would be best kung may ganong mekanism po.

**[02:27:50.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002225)  
Baka po kung may report lang din yung duty-free recent na mga na-partner niya with regional businesses,

**[02:27:58.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002226)  
local businesses.

**[02:27:59.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002227)  
Kung maaari ay masubmit din po yun.

**[02:28:01.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002228)  
That's all, Madam Chair. Thank you very much for answering my questions.

**[02:28:05.520] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002229)  
Salamat po, Madam Chair. We will provide the necessary information po.

**[02:28:10.680] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-002230)
